Question 178 (PHY-2021-0831-S2-Q001) 2021 Shift 2 MCQ

Statement I: If three forces →𝐹1, →𝐹2 and →𝐹3 are represented by three sides of a triangle and →𝐹1 + →𝐹2 = - →𝐹3, then these three forces are concurrent forces and satisfy the condition for equilibrium.
